13e:T31dc,<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line" data-line="1"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> numpy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="2"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> numpy.polynomial </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Polynomial</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="3"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="4"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">np.random.seed(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="5"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">X </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.sort(np.random.rand(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="6"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">y </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.cos(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.pi </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> X) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.random.randn(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.1</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="7"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="8"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> kfold_cv</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(x, y, degree, S):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="9"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    N </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> len</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(x)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="10" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    sizes </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.full(S, N </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> S)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    sizes[: N </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">%</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> S] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D">                 # 前 N mod S 折各多一个点</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    starts </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.concatenate([[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], np.cumsum(sizes)])</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="13"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    errors </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> []</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="14"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> range</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(S):                  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># run 1 … run S</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="15" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        val </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.arange(starts[s], starts[s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">])</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="16" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        train </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.setdiff1d(np.arange(N), val)   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 其余 S−1 折</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="17"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D">        # Polynomial.fit 先把 x 缩放到 [−1, 1] 再做最小二乘，数值上比直接用 x^j 稳</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="18"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        p </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Polynomial.fit(x[train], y[train], degree)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="19" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        errors.append(np.mean((p(x[val]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> y[val]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.mean(errors), np.std(errors), errors</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> degree </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">15</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    m, s, errs </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> kfold_cv(X, y, degree, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">S</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">    print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"degree </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">degree</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:2d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">: CV MSE = </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.2e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> (+/- </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.2e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">)   第 1 折 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">errs[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.2e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">m_loo, _, _ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> kfold_cv(X, y, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">S</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">len</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(X))      </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># S = N：留一法</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"degree  4 留一法: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">m_loo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.2e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 输出：</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="30"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># degree  1: CV MSE = 4.08e-01 (+/- 4.25e-01)   第 1 折 1.15e+00</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="31"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># degree  4: CV MSE = 4.32e-02 (+/- 7.08e-02)   第 1 折 2.52e-01</span></span>
